- **Requirement / Work Item / Type:** `CPF-FR-017267` / `CPF-WP-OPS-TOPOLOGY-05-OPERATIONS_SECURITY` / `DIRECT_VERIFICATION`
- **Actor / Preconditions / Test data:** 개발GPT 자체검수·Codex·QA | 최신 origin/master exact SHA, clean tree, Owner/Consumer inventory, 필요한 Runtime 준비 | ONLINE::Runtime Group 정상·오류·경계 데이터
- **Steps:** 1) change_target 구현을 Source/API/SQL/Config/Frontend/Script에서 추적한다. 2) actual_consumer 경로를 실제 실행한다. 3) acceptance_criteria를 항목별로 확인한다. 4) verification_method를 실행한다. 5) regression_protection과 완료 금지 조건을 검사한다.
- **Expected / Failure criteria:** Runtime Group의 Expected Version가 실제 API·DB·권한·상태·Audit와 연결되고 정상·빈결과·권한부족·충돌·부분실패·세션만료에서 다음 행동을 알 수 있어야 한다. Browser build만 성공하거나 Mock/Raw JSON이면 실패다. | Menu/Route/빈 Component만 존재, Raw JSON Primary UI, Client 전체 Paging, UI-only 권한, Partial을 Empty로 표시, PII 원문 기본, Browser 미검증
- **Source / Consumer / Full call path:** cpf-batch/contract/src/main/java/com/cpf/batch/api/RuntimeRegistration.java; cpf-batch/contract/src/main/java/com/cpf/batch/api/RuntimeHeartbeat.java; cpf-batch/contract/src/main/java/com/cpf/batch/api/RuntimeRole.java; cpf-batch/control-server/src/main/java/com/cpf/batch/control/internal/JdbcRuntimeRegistry.java; cpf-admin/src/main/java/com/cpf/admin/opr/controller/AdmServiceRegistryController.java; cpf-admin/src/main/java/com/cpf/admin/opr/service/AdmServiceRegistryService.java | All runtimes register; ADM topology/operations consume registry | Runtime registration/heartbeat -> JdbcRuntimeRegistry -> desired/actual grouping -> ADM service registry/topology
- **Behavior / Test / Assertion:** System/instance/role/group identity and heartbeat are durable and queryable. | Duplicate/stale registration and version conflict are explicit. | TTL expiry/re-registration/reconcile preserves fencing/version. | runtime registry/state provider tests; ADM service registry tests; multi-instance process runtime pending | exact source SHA·clean tree·command·environment·start/end·exit code·actual output·report/artifact SHA-256·sanitization | CPF-SC-027788 DIRECT_VERIFICATION: the linked requirement acceptance must be directly traced to source, consumer, assertion and evidence; expected=Runtime Group의 Expected Version가 실제 API·DB·권한·상태·Audit와 연결되고 정상·빈결과·권한부족·충돌·부분실패·세션만료에서 다음 행동을 알 수 있어야 한다. Browser build만 성공하거나 Mock/Raw JSON이면 실패다.; failure=Menu/Route/빈 Component만 존재, Raw JSON Primary UI, Client 전체 Paging, UI-only 권한, Partial을 Empty로 표시, PII 원문 기본, Browser 미검증
- **Command / Exit / Actual:** `GitHub Connector exact-SHA source/consumer inventory + local CSV contract reconstruction` / `0 (source/ledger reconstruction); target tools separated` / CPF-SC-027788 개별 판정: 재확인 필요/재확인 필요; DIRECT_VERIFICATION Acceptance·Steps·Expected·Failure Criteria·Assertion을 전용 anchor에 기록; 직접 Harness 또는 exact-SHA source reconstruction 근거, 목표환경 차이는 재확인 필요
